This work is largely based on the v3.02 dump by luckytyphlosion.
He has also helped a great amount pointing me to the right direction to make the necessary changes to update the dump script.
Without his help this dump would take too long and I probably had given up halfway.

Some changes compared to the v3.02 dump:

-   Manually fix a bunch of pokemon names to match it to their showdown name.
    This means you should be able to import the set to the calulator.
    If you see a mon with totally wrong name that's because I messed up.
-   Change the max level check algorithm.
    It should be more accurate but not 100% accurate, it still gets a lot of it wrong especially for bosses.
    Check the official doc!
-   Change the algorithm which checks for pokemon gender to be more accurate.
    This is niche but probably useful for some people who run mons with Cute Charm or Rivalry.
-   Removed the Stats line. I think if you are going to paste this into the calc anyway, it doesn't matter.
    And it makes it easier for me to simply remove the EVs line to make the min grind paste from the non-min grind paste
    without having to recalculate the Stats.
-   Add type to mons with Hidden Power move.

Change compared to the v4.00 dump:
-   Translate the magic numbers based Jwow's pokedex. This should make the dump more accurate.

If there are any inaccuracies in this dump, bug me on Discord at rudo2204.
This is a mingrind paste. It is derived from the non-mingrind paste by removing the EVs line.
